# Consolidated changes against witnesses C and H

Readings were adjudicated against the supplied scan images. A row is a local evidential place; a row may differ from both witnesses. Macro syntax and editorial page-marker conversions are excluded. Resolution of an existing doubt is recorded and separately identified; newly added uncertainty without a new reading is listed in the doubts file.

## Consequential mathematical corrections

- **P025-001**, scan 025, top boxed formula, second argument: `\unsure{d&#124;f}` / `fY` → `d`. The letter has a closed lower oval with a tall right ascender and no transverse bar. Both following f forms have a crossing stroke and/or a descender; the left argument is d-shaped.
- **P025-005**, scan 025, prose opening, membership: `\unsure{\Gamma\Omega&#124;\Omega&#124;\Gamma(\Omega)}` / `\Gamma(\Omega)` → `\Gamma\Omega`. After the membership sign there is a vertical Gamma with a rightward top bar, immediately followed by Omega. No surrounding parentheses occur.
- **P025-008**, scan 025, first formula, crossed glyph before Z: `\deleted{\unsure{Y}}` / `∅` → `\deleted{\unsure{Y}}`. A fork and long descender occur below the comma/left edge of Z and are crossed by the rising correction stroke. The older letter is not completely separable from that stroke.
- **P025-009**, scan 025, first formula, omega following the corrected Z: `\deleted{\unsure{\omega}}` / `∅` → `\omega`. Two small rounded omega humps survive between the heavy Z and the closing angle. No strike can be traced through those humps; the heavy Z strokes stop to their left. Glyph is readable; whether the author meant this residual omega to survive remains uncertain.
- **P025-010**, scan 025, first formula, crossed contraction after minus ThetaZ: `\deleted{\unsure{i_{[X,Y]}}}` / `∅` → `\deleted{\unsure{i_{[X,Y]}}}`. A dotted/hooked i and portions of a low bracketed subscript remain under diagonal hatching. The individual subscript letters are not all recoverable.
- **P025-011**, scan 025, second formula, pairing following operator commutator: `\langle\omega,Z\rangle` / `\langle \omega, Z \rangle` → `\unsure{\langle}\omega,\deleted{\unsure{Y}}Z\rangle`. Between omega and the retraced Z there is an additional forked descending glyph crossed by a slanted correction, matching the older Y-like trace in the preceding display. Neither witness records it.
- **P025-012**, scan 025, fourth calculation, old operator beneath delta iZ: `\delta\deleted{\unsure{\Theta_Z}}i_Z` / `\delta i_Z` → `\underset{\deleted{\unsure{\Theta_Z}}}{\delta i_Z}`. The old oval body and low Z-shaped index lie below the live delta iZ, under hatching and a short horizontal stroke; the cancelled material is not on the live baseline.
- **P025-013**, scan 025, fourth calculation, sign before minus ThetaX: `\deleted{\unsure{+}}` / `∅` → `\deleted{\unsure{+}}`. A compact blackened patch precedes the minus. A short downward remnant could be the old plus stem, but hatching prevents recovery.
- **P025-014**, scan 025, fourth calculation, cancelled delta between iY and iZ: `\deleted{\unsure{\delta}}` / `∅` → `\deleted{\delta}`. Below the diagonal cancellation there remains a closed bottom loop and rising stem, matching the uncancelled delta immediately to the right of iZ.
- **P025-015**, scan 025, fourth calculation, extent of square underbracket: `\Theta_Y i_X i_Z}\delta\omega` / `\theta_Y i_X i_Z \delta\omega` → `\Theta_Y i_X i_Z\delta\omega}`. The underbracket extends to the right below the second delta-omega, ending in an upright beneath its omega. C leaves this delta-omega outside the underbracket.
- **P025-017**, scan 025, fifth calculation, delta immediately after ThetaZ: `\Theta_Z\deleted{\unsure{\delta}}` / `\theta_Z i_X i_Y` → `\Theta_Z\deleted{\delta}`. The small cancelled sign retains the angular upper return and the closed lower loop of delta, with cancellation diagonals across its stem.
- **P025-018**, scan 025, fifth calculation, delta immediately after iY: `i_X i_Y\deleted{\unsure{\delta}}` / `\theta_Z i_X i_Y` → `i_X i_Y\deleted{\delta}`. The second cancelled delta likewise retains its lower loop, below two oblique strikes; it is a distinct sign following the iY subscript.
- **P025-019**, scan 025, fifth calculation, cancelled sign before minus ThetaX: `\deleted{\unsure{+}}-\Theta_X` / `∅` → `\deleted{\unsure{+}}-\Theta_X`. An oblong blackened patch occupies a separate sign position. A small lower protrusion suggests plus but does not establish the full sign.
- **P025-021**, scan 025, boxed Cartan identity in crossed panel: `\Theta_{\unsure{Z&#124;X}}` / `\theta_X = i_X \delta` → `\Theta_{\unsure{Z&#124;X}}`. The subscript has both a top/bottom horizontal and crossing diagonals. These may represent an overtraced Z or X; the two clear iX factors do not settle the ink.
- **P025-024**, scan 025, first crossed calculation, older cancelled triple: `\unsure{\delta i_X i_Y i_Z}` / `\struck{\ill{}}` → `\deleted{\unsure{\delta i_X i_Y i_X&#124;\delta i_X i_Y i_Z}}`. The last surviving low letter in the crossed original has two diagonal arms meeting as X, whereas its earlier indices are entangled in hatching. The upper replacement is separately legible as delta iZ iY iX.
- **P025-025**, scan 025, first crossed calculation, sign before iX delta iY iZ: `\inserted{\delta i_Z i_Y i_X}-i_X` / `= i_X \delta i_Y i_Z` → `\inserted{\delta i_Z i_Y i_X}\unsure{-&#124;=}i_X`. - **P025-026**, scan 025, crossed continuation, locally cancelled plus: `\unsure{+}+i_Y` / `+ i_Y \delta i_X i_Z` → `\deleted{+}+i_Y`. Before the live plus a second sign has a distinct crossbar and projecting lower vertical, with short oblique cancellation strokes over it.
- **P025-027**, scan 025, second crossed calculation, old sign before minus iX: `\unsure{+}-i_X` / `∅` → `\deleted{\unsure{+&#124;\delta}}-i_X`. A narrow hatched upright sign precedes the unambiguous minus; its curved lower remnant does not uniquely distinguish the old plus from a delta-like form.
- **P025-028**, scan 025, second crossed calculation, sign before 3 delta: `)-3\delta` / `= 3\, \delta` → `)\unsure{-&#124;=}3\delta`. The sign before 3 is another compact blackened horizontal patch. Its inner slit permits an old equals but the dark final horizontal can also be a minus.
- **P025-029**, scan 025, last line of crossed panel, minus before 9: `=-9\,\delta` / `\struck{9\, \delta` → `=-9\,\delta`. A short thick minus lies directly before the loop-and-descender 9, distinguishable beneath the long cancellation/boundary stroke.
- **P025-032**, scan 025, individually struck cyclic identity, disputed sign: `\unsure{+&#124;-}` / `= -\sum` → `\unsure{-&#124;+}`. The horizontal strike passes exactly through the sign position. A separate complete plus upright cannot be recovered; the visible horizontal remnant favours minus, while plus remains possible.
- **P025-033**, scan 025, bottom T expression, hatched sign before ThetaZ: `\deleted{\unsure{\delta}}` / `∅` → `\deleted{\unsure{\delta&#124;i_Z}}`. The narrow cancellation has a looped lower remnant and an additional low Z-like tail; neither a complete delta nor a dotted i plus subscript can be disentangled.
- **P025-035**, scan 025, bottom struck equality after T expression: `\deleted{\unsure{=\sum i_X i_Y}}` / `\struck{= \ill{}}` → `\deleted{\unsure{=\frac{1}{2}i_{X\wedge Y}}}`. The earlier summation candidate does not account for the raised numerator. The entire cancelled expression remains qualified because the wavy strike obscures part of the construction.
- **P026-004**, scan 026, deleted tail of first T display: `\deleted{\unsure{-\delta i_Z i_X i_Y}}` / `\struck{\ill{}}` → `\deleted{\unsure{-\delta i_Z i_X i_Y}}`. - **P026-008**, scan 026, first line of main paragraph: `\uuline{$\Theta$-Algèbre de Lie}` / `\emph{$\mathcal{O}$-Algèbre de Lie}` → `\uuline{$\Theta$-Algèbre de Lie}`. The first oval is crossed through its middle, distinguishing Θ from the open-centred ring O elsewhere. Two independent long strokes underline the phrase.

The final Z has a heavy diagonal plus a thin lower-left return which might be a deliberate doubling; the image does not securely distinguish plain from double-struck Z.

The underlying oval suggests Θ, but cancellation prevents a certain full reading; its deletion layer is nevertheless present and omitted by H.

- initial bi-catégorie, final e cell: `\deleted{\unsure{i}}e`; alternatives `An old i is favoured by the high rounded upright/dot and surviving stem; lower structure is fused with clear e.`; medium confidence.
- bi-semisimpliciaux, second m: `\deleted{\unsure{p}}m`; alternatives `p is favoured by the descender and foot, but the upper loop is hidden by m.`; medium confidence.
- s’interprète, first two cells: `\unsure{\deleted{'}s\deleted{s}'}interprète`; alternatives `Both cells carry s and an apostrophe-like mark; the proposed reciprocal correction and first prior punctuation mark are less secure than the presence of the superpositions.`; medium confidence.
- array, first row middle upper index: `X^{\deleted{\unsure{o1&#124;d1}}}_{01}`; alternatives `Old o1 preferred; d1 remains possible because the first oval carries a high vertical/serif or cancellation stroke. Lower handwritten 01 is clear.`; medium confidence.

### Remaining unresolved places

- Top marginal initial sign: `\unsure{!}`; alternatives `Exclamation/attention sign or nonlexical reference sign; shaft and small lower loop are definite.`; medium confidence.
- Typed canceled final cell after défini: `\unsure{\deleted{d}s&#124;d}`; alternatives `A definite d-like ascender/bowl with possibly superposed s; the two alternatives keep the unresolved overtype interpretation.`; medium confidence.
- Canceled subscript cell after the first C_o: `\deleted{$_{\unsure{u&#124;a}}$}`; alternatives `Old u is favored by paired uprights and a low join; a remains possible under the keyed cancellation.`; medium confidence.
- After Quand on explicite, R00375: `l\unsure{\deleted{e}a&#124;\deleted{a}e}`; alternatives `Standing a over e versus standing e over a in one cell.`; low confidence.
- After construction chérie, R00376: `d\unsure{\deleted{a}e&#124;\deleted{e}a}`; alternatives `Standing e over a versus standing a over e in the same cell; de first because chronology is unresolved and both fit the visible strokes.`; low confidence.
- Handwritten insertion, R00378: `\unsure{simplifie}`; alternatives `Simplifie is favored by two i dots and the tall l/f shapes; light initial joins remain uncertain.`; medium confidence.
- Quoted handwritten category name: `\unsure{Grab}`; alternatives `Final b is definite; the crowded middle oval favors a but does not allow complete certainty.`; medium confidence.
- Both handwritten Pi terms: `\unsure{\Pi&#124;\pi}_0\,\unsure{\Pi&#124;\pi}_1`; alternatives `Flat top bars and straight paired stems favor capitals; curved feet leave lower-case π possible.`; medium confidence.
- First words of canceled oblique note, R00380: `\unsure{Quillen dit}`; alternatives `Letter sequence is favored but long vertical cancellation strokes obscure several joins.`; low confidence.
- Tail of canceled oblique note, R00381: `\unsure{la fin} \unsure{de} \illegible{1}`; alternatives `Tall initial/low oval and f-like crossed stem favor la fin; middle de remains qualified; the remaining crossed group is unreadable.`; low confidence.
- Live oblique note after Mais: `\unsure{attention&#124;alors}`; alternatives `Small first rounded form, two tall crossed/looping strokes and compressed ending favor attention; faint joins leave alors possible.`; low confidence.
- Canceled typed cluster before classification: `\deleted{\unsure{interp}}`; alternatives `Six typed cells favor i/n/t/e/r/p; the initial dot and joins are crossed by the manuscript sont and keyed cancellation.`; medium confidence.
- After le sens, R00386: `d\unsure{\deleted{a}e&#124;\deleted{e}a}`; alternatives `Standing e over a versus standing a over e in the same cell; de first because chronology is unresolved and both fit the visible strokes.`; low confidence.
- Underletter in the a of d’associativité: `\deleted{\unsure{i}}`; alternatives `A dotted-i-like vertical survives beneath a, but its full foot is obscured.`; medium confidence.
- Bottom canceled marginal word: `\deleted{\uuline{\unsure{pas vrai&#124;pourquoi}}}`; alternatives `The separated p/a/s-like and v/r/a/i-like groups favor pas vrai; heavy crossing leaves pourquoi possible. Two lower underlines remain visible.`; low confidence.
- Second cell of canceled sent: `\deleted{s\unsure{e&#124;o}nt}`; alternatives `e first; o possible because cancellation coincides with the apparent middle bar.`; medium confidence.
- Handwritten parenthesis between H and Pi terms, R00379: `\unsure{qui jouent&#124;ou l'invariant}`; alternatives `qui jouent first; ou l’invariant retained because the machine and manuscript strokes overlap.`; low confidence.
- Name-like group after les résultats de in canceled marginal note: `\unsure{Verdier}`; alternatives `Verdier remains a candidate; crossed joins do not establish a complete competing name or phrase.`; low confidence.

- L18 under opening parenthesis of Bicat: `\deleted{\unsure{d}}(Bicat)`; alternatives `Old d preferred from upright, serif and lower oval; other underletter not securely separable.`; medium confidence.

### Remaining unresolved places

- Top insertion article: `\unsure{la&#124;le}`; alternatives `The final reduced form can be read a or e; la is preferred with the feminine noun formule.`; medium confidence.
- Line 3, d/e layer order: `l\unsure{\deleted{d}e&#124;\deleted{e}d}s`; alternatives `les with deleted d; contrary order lds with deleted e. Both impressions occupy one cell.`; medium confidence.
- Canceled cluster before the first functor: `\deleted{$\unsure{B&#124;C}\quad\underline{B}$}`; alternatives `First capital B or C; intervening pen strokes do not establish an arrow or readable additional letter.`; low confidence.
- Old impressions below live On: `\deleted{\unsure{I}}O\deleted{\unsure{l}}n`; alternatives `Old Il is plausible from tall straight serif forms, but either underletter may remain unidentified. On itself is live.`; medium confidence.
- Second composite label, p/q layer order: `\unsure{\deleted{p}q&#124;\deleted{q}p}`; alternatives `q over p preferred; reverse chronology p over q retained.`; medium confidence.
- Line 22, shared l/a cell: `remp\unsure{\deleted{l}a&#124;\deleted{a}l}çant`; alternatives `rempaçant with deleted l; remplçant with deleted a. Neither is completed by adding a missing machine cell.`; medium confidence.
- Tail of canceled application phrase: `\deleted{application $T$ \unsure{une}}`; alternatives `une is the candidate supplied by the u-cup, n-arch and e bowl/bar; the dense x cancellation limits certainty.`; medium confidence.
- Line 26, shared h/n cell: `poi\unsure{\deleted{h}n&#124;\deleted{n}h}ts`; alternatives `points with deleted h preferred; poihts with deleted n retained if the opposite layer order was typed.`; medium confidence.
- Top insertion, final word: `\unsure{formule&#124;formules}`; alternatives `The terminal downward return after e can include s, but does not settle a separate final letter; singular is preferred.`; medium confidence.
- Line 10, canceled character immediately after B: `\deleted{$B$\unsure{(}}`; alternatives `A parenthesis-shaped curve survives through cancellation; opening parenthesis is plausible, cancellation penwork remains an alternative explanation.`; medium confidence.
- Final insertion, last letter of dans: `dan\unsure{s} $\overline{T}$`; alternatives `The terminal low bend is compatible with s but is not clearly separable from the preceding cursive return.`; medium confidence.
